## Idea

The
{{#concept "standard strict ordering" Disambiguation="real numbers" Agda=le-ℝ}}
on the [real numbers](real-numbers.dedekind-real-numbers.md) is defined as the
presence of a [rational number](elementary-number-theory.rational-numbers.md)
between them. This is the definition used in {{#cite UF13}}, section 11.2.1.

## Properties

### Strict inequality on the reals implies inequality

<pre class="Agda"><a id="2854" class="Keyword">module</a> <a id="2861" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#2861" class="Module">_</a>
  <a id="2865" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="2866" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#2866" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="2869" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#2869" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="2872" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="2874" href="Agda.Primitive.html#742" class="Postulate">Level</a><a id="2879" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="2881" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="2882" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#2882" class="Bound">x</a> <a id="2884" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="2886" href="real-numbers.dedekind-real-numbers.html#4019" class="Function">ℝ</a> <a id="2888" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#2866" class="Bound">l1</a><a id="2890" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="2892" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="2893" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#2893" class="Bound">y</a> <a id="2895" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="2897" href="real-numbers.dedekind-real-numbers.html#4019" class="Function">ℝ</a> <a id="2899" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#2869" class="Bound">l2</a><a id="2901" class="Symbol">)</a>
  <a id="2905" class="Keyword">where</a>

  <a id="2914" class="Keyword">opaque</a>
    <a id="2925" class="Keyword">unfolding</a> <a id="2935" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#2435" class="Function">le-ℝ</a> <a id="2940" href="real-numbers.inequality-real-numbers.html#2431" class="Function">leq-ℝ</a>

    <a id="2951" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#2951" class="Function">leq-le-ℝ</a> <a id="2960" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="2962" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#2435" class="Function">le-ℝ</a> <a id="2967" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#2882" class="Bound">x</a> <a id="2969" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#2893" class="Bound">y</a> <a id="2971" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="2973" href="real-numbers.inequality-real-numbers.html#2431" class="Function">leq-ℝ</a> <a id="2979" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#2882" class="Bound">x</a> <a id="2981" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#2893" class="Bound">y</a>

### Strict inequality on the reals is irreflexive

<pre class="Agda"><a id="3233" class="Keyword">module</a> <a id="3240" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#3240" class="Module">_</a>
  <a id="3244" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="3245" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#3245" class="Bound">l</a> <a id="3247" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="3249" href="Agda.Primitive.html#742" class="Postulate">Level</a><a id="3254" class="Symbol">}</a>
  <a id="3258" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="3259" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#3259" class="Bound">x</a> <a id="3261" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="3263" href="real-numbers.dedekind-real-numbers.html#4019" class="Function">ℝ</a> <a id="3265" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#3245" class="Bound">l</a><a id="3266" class="Symbol">)</a>
  <a id="3270" class="Keyword">where</a>

  <a id="3279" class="Keyword">opaque</a>
    <a id="3290" class="Keyword">unfolding</a> <a id="3300" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#2435" class="Function">le-ℝ</a>

### The canonical map from rationals to reals preserves and reflects strict inequality

<pre class="Agda"><a id="4722" class="Keyword">module</a> <a id="4729" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#4729" class="Module">_</a>
  <a id="4733" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="4734" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#4734" class="Bound">x</a> <a id="4736" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#4736" class="Bound">y</a> <a id="4738" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="4740" href="elementary-number-theory.rational-numbers.html#2278" class="Function">ℚ</a><a id="4741" class="Symbol">)</a>

  <a id="4754" class="Keyword">opaque</a>
    <a id="4765" class="Keyword">unfolding</a> <a id="4775" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#2435" class="Function">le-ℝ</a> <a id="4780" href="real-numbers.rational-real-numbers.html#2482" class="Function">real-ℚ</a>

    <a id="4792" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#4792" class="Function">preserves-le-real-ℚ</a> <a id="4812" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="4814" href="elementary-number-theory.strict-inequality-rational-numbers.html#3164" class="Function">le-ℚ</a> <a id="4819" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#4734" class="Bound">x</a> <a id="4821" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#4736" class="Bound">y</a> <a id="4823" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="4825" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#2435" class="Function">le-ℝ</a> <a id="4830" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="4831" href="real-numbers.rational-real-numbers.html#2482" class="Function">real-ℚ</a> <a id="4838" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#4734" class="Bound">x</a><a id="4839" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="4841" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="4842" href="real-numbers.rational-real-numbers.html#2482" class="Function">real-ℚ</a> <a id="4849" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#4736" class="Bound">y</a><a id="4850" class="Symbol">)</a>
    <a id="4856" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#4792" class="Function">preserves-le-real-ℚ</a> <a id="4876" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#4876" class="Bound">x&lt;y</a> <a id="4880" class="Symbol">=</a>
      <a id="4888" href="foundation.existential-quantification.html#4482" class="Function">intro-exists</a>

  <a id="11402" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="11403" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#11403" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="11406" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#11406" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="11409" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="11411" href="Agda.Primitive.html#742" class="Postulate">Level</a><a id="11416" class="Symbol">}</a>

### Strict inequality on the real numbers is cotransitive

<pre class="Agda"><a id="11753" class="Keyword">opaque</a>
  <a id="11762" class="Keyword">unfolding</a> <a id="11772" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#2435" class="Function">le-ℝ</a>

  <a id="cotransitive-le-ℝ"></a><a id="11780" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#11780" class="Function">cotransitive-le-ℝ</a> <a id="11798" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="11800" href="foundation.large-binary-relations.html#4821" class="Function">is-cotransitive-Large-Relation-Prop</a> <a id="11836" href="real-numbers.dedekind-real-numbers.html#4019" class="Function">ℝ</a> <a id="11838" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#2687" class="Function">le-prop-ℝ</a>

### If `x < y`, then there is some `ε : ℚ⁺` with `x + ε < y`

### If `x` is less than each rational number `y` is less than, then `x ≤ y`

<pre class="Agda"><a id="21059" class="Keyword">module</a> <a id="21066" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#21066" class="Module">_</a>

### Two real numbers are similar if they are less than the same rational numbers

<pre class="Agda"><a id="21498" class="Keyword">module</a> <a id="21505" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#21505" class="Module">_</a>
  <a id="21509" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="21510" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#21510" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="21513" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#21513" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="21516" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="21518" href="Agda.Primitive.html#742" class="Postulate">Level</a><a id="21523" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="21525" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="21526" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#21526" class="Bound">x</a> <a id="21528" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="21530" href="real-numbers.dedekind-real-numbers.html#4019" class="Function">ℝ</a> <a id="21532" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#21510" class="Bound">l1</a><a id="21534" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="21536" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="21537" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#21537" class="Bound">y</a> <a id="21539" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="21541" href="real-numbers.dedekind-real-numbers.html#4019" class="Function">ℝ</a> <a id="21543" href="real-numbers.strict-inequality-real-numbers.html#21513" class="Bound">l2</a><a id="21545" class="Symbol">)</a>
  <a id="21549" class="Keyword">where</a>
